Change 455068: [SW-190823] g3: add compressed bist table in spi

Project: buildroot-external

Status: NEW

Created: 2024-10-08 17:53:02.136000000

Updated: 2025-04-14 13:02:40.177000000

Commit Message

[SW-190823] g3: add compressed bist table in spi

This patch adds minilzo compressed engine bist table
in spi flash. This compressed data will be decompressed
at preboot stage using minilzo. The decompressed data is
used for engine bist.

First entry represents compressed size. It is used in
preboot for decompressing.

Change-Id: I35043a24cf370d96313604d9c1a6122d8d57f640
Signed-off-by: Tamal <tasaha@habana.ai>
